      BEGIN 25785
      Integer parameter channel (IP)
      Name=Max_diffrential
      Description=
      Unit=%
      Mode selector=Not used
      Default mode={70}
        Default value=70
      X=210
      Y=80
      END 25785
      
      BEGIN 25786
      Math channel (MAC)
      Name=Left unfree
      Description=
      Unit=Not used
      Function selector=Not used
      X=400
      Y=150
        
        BEGIN 25790
        Function
        Name=Default function
        Description=
        Qcode expression:
          right := DMAC_EV_1_Right
          left := DMAC_EV_2_Left
          
          if (right > 0 and left < 0) or (right < 0 and left > 0) then
            res := 0
          elseif  Abs(right) <= Abs(left) then
            res := left
          elseif Abs(right) > Abs(left) then
            res := MaxOfAbs(right * Max_diffrential  / 100, left)
          endif
          
          
          Result := res
        END 25790
      END 25786

      BEGIN 25880
      Math channel (MAC)
      Name=Right unfree
      Description=
      Unit=Not used
      Function selector=Not used
      X=400
      Y=110
        
        BEGIN 25884
        Function
        Name=Default function
        Description=
        Qcode expression:
          right := DMAC_EV_1_Right
          left := DMAC_EV_2_Left
          
          if (right > 0 and left < 0) or (right < 0 and left > 0) then
            res := 0
          elseif  Abs(right) >= Abs(left) then
            res := right
          elseif Abs(right) < Abs(left) then
            res := MaxOfAbs(left * Max_diffrential  / 100, right)
          endif
          
          
          Result := res
        END 25884
      END 25880
